Therefore, decision calibration is a special instance of
\mathcal{W}_{\mathrm{dec}}
-calibration for

\mathcal{W}_{\mathrm{dec}}:=\{w_{\ell,\ell^{\prime}}:w_{\ell,\ell^{\prime}}(p(x))=\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell}(a)\tilde{k}_{f_{p},\ell^{\prime}}(x,a)\forall\ell,\ell^{\prime}\in\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{H}}\}.

6.1.2
Patching in the Infinite-dimensional Setting

The first challenge in the infinite-dimensional setting is that we need to restrict the predictor in the form of
Eq.

6
so that we can use the reproducing property to construct a loss estimator
f_{p}
. Therefore, in each round
t
, once we find
\ell_{t},\ell_{t}^{\prime}
that violates the decision calibration, we cannot directly follow the original weighted calibration algorithm template to update the predictor by patching
w_{\ell_{t},\ell_{t}^{\prime}}

unless

r_{\ell_{t}}(a)
can be explicitly expressed by the linear combination of
\phi(y)
.

However, note that

\displaystyle{\mathchoice{\left|\mathbb{E}{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p_{t}(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p_{t}(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p_{t}(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p_{t}(x)\rangle}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p_{t}(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p_{t}(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p_{t}(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p_{t}(x)\rangle}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p_{t}(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p_{t}(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p_{t}(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p_{t}(x)\rangle}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p_{t}(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p_{t}(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p_{t}(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p_{t}(x)\rangle}}]}}\right|}{|\mathbb{E}{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ \displaystyle={\mathchoice{\left|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}{\mathchoice{\left\langle r_{\ell_{t}}(a),\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\right\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell_{t}}(a),\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell_{t}}(a),\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell_{t}}(a),\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\rangle}}\right|}{|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}{\mathchoice{\left\langle r_{\ell_{t}}(a),\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\right\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell_{t}}(a),\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell_{t}}(a),\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell_{t}}(a),\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\rangle}}|}{|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}{\mathchoice{\left\langle r_{\ell_{t}}(a),\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\right\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell_{t}}(a),\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell_{t}}(a),\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell_{t}}(a),\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\rangle}}|}{|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}{\mathchoice{\left\langle r_{\ell_{t}}(a),\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\right\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell_{t}}(a),\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell_{t}}(a),\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell_{t}}(a),\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\rangle}}|}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}R_{1}{\mathchoice{\left\|\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\right\|}{\|\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\|}{\|\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\|}{\|\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}.

The inequality becomes equality when
r_{\ell^{*}_{t}}(a)=R_{1}\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]/{\mathchoice{\left\|\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\right\|}{\|\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\|}{\|\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\|}{\|\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}
. On the one hand, once we identify some pair of
\ell_{t},\ell_{t}^{\prime}
, replacing
\ell_{t}
with
\ell_{t}^{*}
will make the violation worse. On the other hand,
r_{\ell^{*}_{t}}(a)
can be expressed by the linear combination of
\phi(y)
(we will use the empirical expectation to approximate the true expectation). Therefore, in each round
t
, we can use
\ell_{t}^{*}
to update the predictor
p_{t}
.

Algorithm 1

DimFreeDeCal

0:

The RKHS kernel
K
, current predictor
p_{0}:\mathcal{X}\rightarrow\mathcal{H}
and tolerance
\epsilon
.

1:

t=0

2:

while

\sup_{\ell,\ell^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}[{\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p_{t}(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p_{t}(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p_{t}(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p_{t}(x)\rangle}}]>\epsilon

do

3:

Find
\ell_{t},{\ell_{t}}^{\prime}
such that
\hat{\mathbb{E}}[{\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p_{t}(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p_{t}(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p_{t}(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p_{t}(x)\rangle}}]>3\epsilon/4

4:

Define the adjustments
d_{ta}=\eta R_{1}\hat{\mathbb{E}}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]/{\mathchoice{\left\|\hat{\mathbb{E}}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\right\|}{\|\hat{\mathbb{E}}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\|}{\|\hat{\mathbb{E}}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\|}{\|\hat{\mathbb{E}}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}
.

5:

Set
p_{t+1}:x\mapsto p_{t}(x)+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)
.

6:

Set
p_{t+1}:x\mapsto\pi_{B(R_{2})}(p_{t+1}(x))
.
//
\pi_{B(R_{2})}
projects onto Hilbert ball
B(R_{2})
with radius
R_{2}

7:

end

while

Intuitively, the algorithm proceeds as follows. In lines 2–3, we invoke the
auditing
oracle: if the loss estimator
f_{p_{0}}
is not
(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{H}},\tilde{K}_{\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{H}}},\epsilon)
-decision calibrated, we can identify a pair
(\ell_{t},\ell_{t}^{\prime})
such that the empirical decision calibration error exceeds
3\epsilon/4
. In line 4, as previously discussed, we substitute
(\ell_{t}^{*},\ell_{t}^{\prime})
for
(\ell_{t},\ell_{t}^{\prime})
to define the patching term so that the updated predictor can remain to be explicitly expressed as a linear combination of
\phi(y)
. Lines 5–6 then carry out the patching step. Notably, we cannot perform computations directly with
p_{t}(x)
, as it may reside in an infinite-dimensional space. To address this, we introduce the technique of
implicit patching
, which we defer to the final part of
Section

6.1.2
. The following theorem shows that
Algorithm

1
satisfies the conditions stated in
Theorem

1.3
.

Theorem 6.1
.

Given any initial predictor
p_{0}
and tolerance
\epsilon
,
Algorithm

1
terminates in
T=O{\mathchoice{\left(\frac{R_{1}^{2}R_{2}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}\right)}{(\frac{R_{1}^{2}R_{2}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}})}{(\frac{R_{1}^{2}R_{2}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}})}{(\frac{R_{1}^{2}R_{2}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}})}}
iterations. Given
\tilde{O}(\frac{1}{\epsilon^{4}})
samples, with probability
1-\delta
,
Algorithm

1
outputs a predictor
p_{T}
such that
f_{p_{T}}
is
(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{H}},\tilde{\mathcal{K}}_{\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{H}}},\epsilon)
-decision calibrated and
\mathbb{E}[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{T}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{T}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{T}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{T}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}^{2}_{\mathcal{H}}]\leq\mathbb{E}[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{0}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{0}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{0}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{0}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}^{2}_{\mathcal{H}}]
.

Proof.

If the algorithm does not terminate at round
t
,we have

\sup_{\ell,\ell^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}]}}>\epsilon.

By uniform convergence property we can find
\ell_{t},\ell_{t}^{\prime}
such that

\hat{\mathbb{E}}{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}]}}>3\epsilon/4.

Let
r_{\ell^{*}_{t}}(a)=R_{1}\hat{E}[(\phi(y)-p(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]/{\mathchoice{\left\|\hat{E}[(\phi(y)-p(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\right\|}{\|\hat{E}[(\phi(y)-p(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\|}{\|\hat{E}[(\phi(y)-p(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\|}{\|\hat{E}[(\phi(y)-p(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\|}}
, by Cauchy inequality we have

\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}{\mathchoice{\left\langle r_{\ell^{*}_{t}}(a),\hat{\mathbb{E}}[(\phi(y)-p(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\right\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell^{*}_{t}}(a),\hat{\mathbb{E}}[(\phi(y)-p(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell^{*}_{t}}(a),\hat{\mathbb{E}}[(\phi(y)-p(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell^{*}_{t}}(a),\hat{\mathbb{E}}[(\phi(y)-p(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\rangle}}\geq\hat{\mathbb{E}}{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}]}}>3\epsilon/4.

Again by uniform convergence,

\mathbb{E}{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell^{*}_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell^{*}_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell^{*}_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell^{*}_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell^{*}_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell^{*}_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell^{*}_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell^{*}_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell^{*}_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell^{*}_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell^{*}_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell^{*}_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell^{*}_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell^{*}_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell^{*}_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell^{*}_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}]}}>\epsilon/2.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}}-\mathbb{E}{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}}

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{2}^{2}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{2}^{2}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{2}^{2}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{2}^{2}]}}-\mathbb{E}{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\right\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\right\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\right\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\right\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\frac{2\eta R_{1}}{{\mathchoice{\left\|\hat{E}[(\phi(y)-p(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\right\|}{\|\hat{E}[(\phi(y)-p(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\|}{\|\hat{E}[(\phi(y)-p(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\|}{\|\hat{E}[(\phi(y)-p(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\|}}}\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]\hat{\mathbb{E}}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]-\mathbb{E}{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\left\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\right\|}{\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}{\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}{\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\right\|}{\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}{\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}{\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\right\|}{\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}{\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}{\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\right\|}{\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}{\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}{\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}}

\displaystyle\geq\eta\epsilon-\mathbb{E}{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\left\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\right\|}{\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}{\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}{\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\right\|}{\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}{\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}{\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\right\|}{\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}{\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}{\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\right\|}{\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}{\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}{\|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}d_{ta}\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}}

\displaystyle\geq\eta\epsilon-\eta^{2}R_{1}^{2}.

Set
\eta=\frac{\epsilon}{2R_{1}^{2}}
, we have

\mathbb{E}{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}}-\mathbb{E}{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}}\geq\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{4R_{1}^{2}}.

Therefore the algorithm will terminate in at most
\frac{16R_{1}^{2}R_{2}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}
because
\mathbb{E}{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{0}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{0}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{0}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{0}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{2}^{2}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{0}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{0}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{0}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{0}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{2}^{2}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{0}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{0}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{0}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{0}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{2}^{2}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{0}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{0}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{0}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{0}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{2}^{2}]}}\leq(2R_{2})^{2}=4R_{2}^{2}
.
∎

Implicit Patching

The second challenge is that we cannot explicitly compute
p_{t}(x)
at each round since the feature space
\mathcal{H}
may be infinite-dimensional. The key idea is to perform the patching implicitly by maintaining a linear representation of the form
p_{t}(x)=\sum_{i=1}^{N_{t}}\alpha_{ti}(x)\phi(y_{ti})
. That is, we keep track of the functions
\alpha_{ti}:\mathcal{X}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
and the corresponding outcomes
y_{ti}
for all
t
and
i\in[N_{t}]
. Given this representation, we can efficiently compute the value of the loss estimator
f_{p_{t}}(x,a,\ell)
for any loss function
\ell\in\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{H}}
as follows

f_{p_{t}}(x,a,\ell)={\mathchoice{\left\langle r_{\ell}(a),p_{t}(x)\right\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),p_{t}(x)\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),p_{t}(x)\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),p_{t}(x)\rangle}}_{\mathcal{H}}=\sum_{i=1}^{N_{t}}\alpha_{ti}(x)\ell(a,y_{ti}).

Formally, we have the following lemma.

Lemma 6.1
.

For
Algorithm

1
, if the input predictor satisfies
p_{0}(x)=\sum_{i=1}^{N_{0}}\alpha_{0i}(x)\phi(y_{0i})
, the for any
t
, we have

p_{t}(x)=\sum_{i=1}^{N_{t}}\alpha_{ti}(x)\phi(y_{ti}).

Proof.

For
Algorithm

1
, the update in round
t
is

p_{t+1}=\pi_{B(R_{2})}(p_{t}(x)+\eta\cdot w_{\ell_{t},\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(p_{t}(x)))
where
w_{\ell_{t},\ell^{\prime}_{t}}
is the patching term. By induction, it suffices to prove that
w_{\ell_{t},\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(p_{t}(x))
can be explicitly represented by the linear combination of
\phi(y)
. Let
S_{t}=\{(x^{\prime}_{ti},y^{\prime}_{ti})\}_{i=1}^{n_{t}}
be the set of samples used for auditing. Then we have

w_{\ell_{t},\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(p_{t}(x))=\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\frac{R_{1}\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x,a)}{{\mathchoice{\left\|\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{t}}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x,a)]\right\|}{\|\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{t}}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x,a)]\|}{\|\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{t}}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x,a)]\|}{\|\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{t}}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x,a)]\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}}\cdot\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{t}}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x,a)]

By induction, we have
p_{t}(x)=\sum_{i=1}^{N_{t}}\alpha_{t,i}(x)\phi(y_{t,i})
. Then we can compute the smooth optimal decision rule
\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}
as

\displaystyle\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x,a)

\displaystyle=\frac{e^{-\beta f_{p_{t}}(x,a,\ell_{t}^{\prime})}}{\sum_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}e^{-\beta f_{p_{t}}(x,a^{\prime},\ell_{t}^{\prime})}}

\displaystyle=\frac{e^{-\beta\sum_{i=1}^{N_{t}}\alpha_{t-1,i}(x)\ell_{t}^{\prime}(a,y_{t,i})}}{\sum_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}e^{-\beta\sum_{i=1}^{N_{t}}\alpha_{t,i}(x)\ell_{t}^{\prime}(a^{\prime},y_{t,i})}}.

Then the norm can be computed as

\displaystyle{\mathchoice{\left\|\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{t}}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x,a)]\right\|}{\|\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{t}}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x,a)]\|}{\|\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{t}}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x,a)]\|}{\|\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{t}}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x,a)]\|}}^{2}_{\mathcal{H}}

\displaystyle={\mathchoice{\left\|\frac{1}{n_{t}}\sum_{i=1}^{n_{t}}(\phi_{y_{ti}^{\prime}}-p_{t}(x^{\prime}_{ti}))\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x^{\prime}_{ti},a)\right\|}{\|\frac{1}{n_{t}}\sum_{i=1}^{n_{t}}(\phi_{y_{ti}^{\prime}}-p_{t}(x^{\prime}_{ti}))\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x^{\prime}_{ti},a)\|}{\|\frac{1}{n_{t}}\sum_{i=1}^{n_{t}}(\phi_{y_{ti}^{\prime}}-p_{t}(x^{\prime}_{ti}))\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x^{\prime}_{ti},a)\|}{\|\frac{1}{n_{t}}\sum_{i=1}^{n_{t}}(\phi_{y_{ti}^{\prime}}-p_{t}(x^{\prime}_{ti}))\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x^{\prime}_{ti},a)\|}}^{2}_{\mathcal{H}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}_{t}}\sum_{i,j\in[n_{t}]}\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x^{\prime}_{ti},a)\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x^{\prime}_{tj},a){\mathchoice{\left\langle\phi_{y^{\prime}_{ti}}-p_{t}(x^{\prime}_{ti})),\phi_{y^{\prime}_{tj}}-p_{t}(x^{\prime}_{tj}))\right\rangle}{\langle\phi_{y^{\prime}_{ti}}-p_{t}(x^{\prime}_{ti})),\phi_{y^{\prime}_{tj}}-p_{t}(x^{\prime}_{tj}))\rangle}{\langle\phi_{y^{\prime}_{ti}}-p_{t}(x^{\prime}_{ti})),\phi_{y^{\prime}_{tj}}-p_{t}(x^{\prime}_{tj}))\rangle}{\langle\phi_{y^{\prime}_{ti}}-p_{t}(x^{\prime}_{ti})),\phi_{y^{\prime}_{tj}}-p_{t}(x^{\prime}_{tj}))\rangle}}_{\mathcal{H}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}_{t}}\sum_{i,j\in[n_{t}]}\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x^{\prime}_{ti},a)\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x^{\prime}_{tj},a)\cdot\Bigg(K(y^{\prime}_{ti},y^{\prime}_{tj})-\sum_{q\in[N_{t}]}\alpha_{tq}(x^{\prime}_{ti})K(y_{tq},y^{\prime}_{tj})

\displaystyle-\sum_{q\in[N_{t}]}\alpha_{tq}(x^{\prime}_{tj})K(y_{tq},y^{\prime}_{ti})+\sum_{q,s\in[N_{t}]}\alpha_{tq}(x^{\prime}_{ti})\alpha_{ts}(x^{\prime}_{tj})K(y_{tq},y_{ts})\Bigg).

Note that the empirical expectation is a linear combination of
\phi(y)
.

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{E}}_{S_{t}}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x,a)]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n_{t}}\sum_{i\in[n_{t}]}(\phi(y^{\prime}_{ti})-p_{t}(x^{\prime}_{ti}))\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x^{\prime}_{ti},a)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n_{t}}\sum_{i\in[n_{t}]}{\mathchoice{\left(\phi(y^{\prime}_{ti})-\sum_{j\in[N_{t}]}\alpha_{tj}(x^{\prime}_{ti})\phi(y_{tj})\right)}{(\phi(y^{\prime}_{ti})-\sum_{j\in[N_{t}]}\alpha_{tj}(x^{\prime}_{ti})\phi(y_{tj}))}{(\phi(y^{\prime}_{ti})-\sum_{j\in[N_{t}]}\alpha_{tj}(x^{\prime}_{ti})\phi(y_{tj}))}{(\phi(y^{\prime}_{ti})-\sum_{j\in[N_{t}]}\alpha_{tj}(x^{\prime}_{ti})\phi(y_{tj}))}}\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x^{\prime}_{ti},a)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in[n_{t}]}\frac{\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x^{\prime}_{ti},a)}{n_{t}}\phi(y^{\prime}_{ti})-\sum_{j\in N_{t}}{\mathchoice{\left(\sum_{i\in[n_{t}]}\frac{\alpha_{tj}(x^{\prime}_{ti})\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x^{\prime}_{ti},a)}{n_{t}}\right)}{(\sum_{i\in[n_{t}]}\frac{\alpha_{tj}(x^{\prime}_{ti})\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x^{\prime}_{ti},a)}{n_{t}})}{(\sum_{i\in[n_{t}]}\frac{\alpha_{tj}(x^{\prime}_{ti})\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x^{\prime}_{ti},a)}{n_{t}})}{(\sum_{i\in[n_{t}]}\frac{\alpha_{tj}(x^{\prime}_{ti})\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x^{\prime}_{ti},a)}{n_{t}})}}\phi(y_{tj}).

Bringing these components together, we know the linear representation of
\Delta_{t}
by
\{\phi(y^{\prime}_{ti})\}_{i=1}^{n_{t}}\bigcup\{\phi(y_{ti})\}_{i=1}^{N_{t}}
can be computed. For ease of notion, we let
\{y_{t+1,i}\}_{i=1}^{N_{t+1}}
to be the union of two set of samples mentioned above. By induction we know that the linear representation of
p_{t}(x)+w_{\ell_{t},\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(p_{t}(x))
can be computed. Let
p_{t}(x)+w_{\ell_{t},\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(p_{t}(x))=\sum_{i\in[N_{t+1}]}\alpha^{\prime}_{t+1,i}(x)\phi(y_{t+1,i})
.

The last thing to show is that after projection
\pi_{B(R_{2})}
, the linear representation can still be computed. We have

\pi_{B(R_{2})}(p_{t+1}(x))=\frac{R_{2}}{{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t+1}(x)\right\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}}\cdot p_{t+1}(x).

The it suffices to show that the norm is computable. We have

\displaystyle{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t+1}(x)\right\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)\|}}^{2}_{\mathcal{H}}

\displaystyle={\mathchoice{\left\langle p_{t+1}(x),p_{t+1}(x)\right\rangle}{\langle p_{t+1}(x),p_{t+1}(x)\rangle}{\langle p_{t+1}(x),p_{t+1}(x)\rangle}{\langle p_{t+1}(x),p_{t+1}(x)\rangle}}_{\mathcal{H}}

\displaystyle={\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{i\in[N_{t+1}]}\alpha^{\prime}_{t+1,i}(x)\phi(y_{t+1,i}),\sum_{i\in[N_{t+1}]}\alpha^{\prime}_{t+1,i}(x)\phi(y_{t+1,i})\right\rangle}{\langle\sum_{i\in[N_{t+1}]}\alpha^{\prime}_{t+1,i}(x)\phi(y_{t+1,i}),\sum_{i\in[N_{t+1}]}\alpha^{\prime}_{t+1,i}(x)\phi(y_{t+1,i})\rangle}{\langle\sum_{i\in[N_{t+1}]}\alpha^{\prime}_{t+1,i}(x)\phi(y_{t+1,i}),\sum_{i\in[N_{t+1}]}\alpha^{\prime}_{t+1,i}(x)\phi(y_{t+1,i})\rangle}{\langle\sum_{i\in[N_{t+1}]}\alpha^{\prime}_{t+1,i}(x)\phi(y_{t+1,i}),\sum_{i\in[N_{t+1}]}\alpha^{\prime}_{t+1,i}(x)\phi(y_{t+1,i})\rangle}}_{\mathcal{H}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,j\in[N_{t+1}]}\alpha^{\prime}_{t+1,i}(x)\alpha^{\prime}_{t+1,j}(x)K(y_{t+1,i},y_{t+1,j}).

∎

6.2
Extension of
Zhao et al. (2021)
’s Algorithm

In this section we provide an extension to
Zhao et al. (2021)
’s algorithm on decision calibration under smoothed optimal decision rule. Our extension also adopts a “patching”-style approach, with the patching component derived from an optimization perspective, following the intuition in their work. Consider that we find the pair of
\ell_{t},\ell_{t}^{\prime}
in round
t
that violates the decision calibration. If we let the patching have the following form

p_{t+1}(x)=p_{t}(x)+U\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x),

we can
heuristically
minimize

\displaystyle L(U):=\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}{\mathchoice{\left\|\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x,a)]\right\|}{\|\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x,a)]\|}{\|\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x,a)]\|}{\|\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x,a)]\|}}^{2}+\lambda{\mathchoice{\left\|U\right\|}{\|U\|}{\|U\|}{\|U\|}}^{2},

(8)

where the first term is trying to decrease the violation of decision calibration and the second term is trying to restrict the norm of
U
so that
U
can be efficiently approximated with samples. By simple calculation we have

\displaystyle L(U)

\displaystyle=\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}{\mathchoice{\left\|G_{a}-(DU^{T})_{a}\right\|}{\|G_{a}-(DU^{T})_{a}\|}{\|G_{a}-(DU^{T})_{a}\|}{\|G_{a}-(DU^{T})_{a}\|}}^{2}+\lambda{\mathchoice{\left\|U\right\|}{\|U\|}{\|U\|}{\|U\|}}^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathchoice{\left\|G-DU^{T}\right\|}{\|G-DU^{T}\|}{\|G-DU^{T}\|}{\|G-DU^{T}\|}}+{\mathchoice{\left\|U\right\|}{\|U\|}{\|U\|}{\|U\|}}^{2}

The optimum of the objective is
U^{*}=G^{T}(D+\lambda I)^{-1}
. Note that the optimization objective of
Zhao et al. (2021)
is just the first term of
Eq.

8
without the second regularization term. Consequently, the optimum becomes
U^{*}=G^{T}D^{-1}
so that the norm of
U^{*}
can be unbounded because the (pseudo)inverse of
D
may not have a bounded norm. Therefore, their algorithm does not have finite sample guarantee. Our regularized extension to their algorithm
Algorithm

2
can fix this problem. In
Algorithm

2
, we choose
\lambda=1
.

Algorithm 2

Finite-Sample Infinite-Dimensional Adaptation of
Zhao et al. (2021)

0:

The RKHS kernel
K
, current predictor
p_{0}:\mathcal{X}\rightarrow\mathcal{H}
and tolerance
\epsilon
.

1:

t=0

2:

while

\exists\ell_{t},{\ell_{t}}^{\prime}
such that
\mathbb{E}[{\mathchoice{\left\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}r_{\ell_{t}}(a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}]>\epsilon

do

3:

Compute
\hat{D}\in\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{A}|\times|\mathcal{A}|}
where
\hat{D}_{aa^{\prime}}=\hat{\mathbb{E}}{\mathchoice{\left[\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a^{\prime})\right]}{[\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a^{\prime})]}{[\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a^{\prime})]}{[\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a^{\prime})]}}
.

4:

Define
\hat{G}\in\mathbb{R}^{K\times\dim(\mathcal{H})}
where
\hat{G}_{a}=\hat{\mathbb{E}}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\tilde{k}_{\ell^{\prime}_{t}}(x,a)]
.

5:

Set
p_{t+1}:x\mapsto\pi_{B(R_{2})}{\mathchoice{\left(p_{t}(x)+\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1}\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x)\right)}{(p_{t}(x)+\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1}\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x))}{(p_{t}(x)+\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1}\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x))}{(p_{t}(x)+\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1}\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x))}}

//
\pi_{B(R_{2})}
projects onto Hilbert ball
B(R_{2})

6:

end

while

The following theorem says that
Algorithm

2
also satisfies the statement of
Theorem

1.3
, but has sample complexity bounds
\tilde{O}(\frac{1}{\epsilon^{6}})
worse than ours
\tilde{O}(\frac{1}{\epsilon^{4}})
.

Theorem 6.2
.

Given any initial predictor
p_{0}
and tolerance
\epsilon
,
Algorithm

2
ends in
T=O{\mathchoice{\left(\frac{R_{1}^{2}R_{2}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}\right)}{(\frac{R_{1}^{2}R_{2}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}})}{(\frac{R_{1}^{2}R_{2}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}})}{(\frac{R_{1}^{2}R_{2}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}})}}
iterations. Given
\tilde{O}(\frac{1}{\epsilon^{6}})
samples, with probability
1-\delta
,
Algorithm

2
outputs a predictor
p_{T}
such that
f_{p_{T}}
is
(\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{H}},\tilde{\mathcal{K}}_{\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{H}}},\epsilon)
-decision calibrated and
\mathbb{E}[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{T}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{T}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{T}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{T}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}^{2}_{\mathcal{H}}]\leq\mathbb{E}[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{0}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{0}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{0}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{0}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}^{2}_{\mathcal{H}}]
.

Proof.

First we have

\displaystyle{\mathchoice{\left\|\hat{G}\right\|}{\|\hat{G}\|}{\|\hat{G}\|}{\|\hat{G}\|}}_{F}\leq 2R_{2}\sqrt{|\mathcal{A}|}.

\displaystyle{\mathchoice{\left\|(\hat{D}+I)^{-1}\right\|}{\|(\hat{D}+I)^{-1}\|}{\|(\hat{D}+I)^{-1}\|}{\|(\hat{D}+I)^{-1}\|}}=\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\sigma_{i}((\hat{D}+I)^{-1})}\leq\sqrt{|\mathcal{A}|}.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}}-\mathbb{E}{\mathchoice{\left[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\right]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}{[{\mathchoice{\left\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\right\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}{\|p_{t+1}(x)-\phi(y)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}]}}

\displaystyle=2\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1}\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x)]-\mathbb{E}[k_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x)^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-T}\hat{G}\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1}k_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x)]

\displaystyle=2\Tr(\mathbb{E}[(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1}\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x)])-\Tr(\mathbb{E}[k_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x)^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-T}\hat{G}\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1}k_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x)])

\displaystyle=2\Tr(\mathbb{E}[\tilde{k}_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x)(\phi(y)-p_{t}(x))\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1}])-\Tr(\mathbb{E}[k_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x)k_{\ell_{t}^{\prime}}(x)^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-T}\hat{G}\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1}])

\displaystyle=2\Tr(G\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1})-\Tr(D(\hat{D}+I)^{-T}\hat{G}\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1})

\displaystyle=2\Tr(G\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1})-\Tr((D+I)(\hat{D}+I)^{-T}\hat{G}\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1})+\Tr((\hat{D}+I)^{-T}\hat{G}\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1})

\displaystyle\geq 2\Tr(G\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1})-\Tr((D+I)(\hat{D}+I)^{-T}\hat{G}\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1})

\displaystyle=2\Tr(\hat{G}\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1})-2\Tr((\hat{G}-G)\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1})

\displaystyle-\Tr((\hat{D}+I)(\hat{D}+I)^{-T}\hat{G}\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1})-\Tr((D-\hat{D})(\hat{D}+I)^{-T}\hat{G}\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1})

\displaystyle=\Tr(\hat{G}\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1})-2\Tr((\hat{G}-G)\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1})-\Tr((D-\hat{D})(\hat{D}+I)^{-T}\hat{G}\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1})

\displaystyle\geq\Tr(\hat{G}\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1})-2{\mathchoice{\left\|\hat{G}-G\right\|}{\|\hat{G}-G\|}{\|\hat{G}-G\|}{\|\hat{G}-G\|}}_{F}{\mathchoice{\left\|\hat{G}^{T}\right\|}{\|\hat{G}^{T}\|}{\|\hat{G}^{T}\|}{\|\hat{G}^{T}\|}}_{F}{\mathchoice{\left\|(\hat{D}+I)^{-1}\right\|}{\|(\hat{D}+I)^{-1}\|}{\|(\hat{D}+I)^{-1}\|}{\|(\hat{D}+I)^{-1}\|}}_{F}-{\mathchoice{\left\|(D-\hat{D})\right\|}{\|(D-\hat{D})\|}{\|(D-\hat{D})\|}{\|(D-\hat{D})\|}}_{F}{\mathchoice{\left\|\hat{G}^{T}\right\|}{\|\hat{G}^{T}\|}{\|\hat{G}^{T}\|}{\|\hat{G}^{T}\|}}^{2}_{F}{\mathchoice{\left\|(\hat{D}+I)^{-1}\right\|}{\|(\hat{D}+I)^{-1}\|}{\|(\hat{D}+I)^{-1}\|}{\|(\hat{D}+I)^{-1}\|}}^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle\geq\Tr(\hat{G}\hat{G}^{T}(\hat{D}+I)^{-1})-4R_{2}|\mathcal{A}|{\mathchoice{\left\|\hat{G}-G\right\|}{\|\hat{G}-G\|}{\|\hat{G}-G\|}{\|\hat{G}-G\|}}_{F}-4R^{2}_{2}|\mathcal{A}|^{2}{\mathchoice{\left\|(D-\hat{D})\right\|}{\|(D-\hat{D})\|}{\|(D-\hat{D})\|}{\|(D-\hat{D})\|}}_{F}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2}\Tr(\hat{G}\hat{G}^{T})-4R_{2}|\mathcal{A}|{\mathchoice{\left\|\hat{G}-G\right\|}{\|\hat{G}-G\|}{\|\hat{G}-G\|}{\|\hat{G}-G\|}}_{F}-4R^{2}_{2}|\mathcal{A}|^{2}{\mathchoice{\left\|(D-\hat{D})\right\|}{\|(D-\hat{D})\|}{\|(D-\hat{D})\|}{\|(D-\hat{D})\|}}_{F}

\displaystyle\geq O{\mathchoice{\left(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{|\mathcal{A}|R^{2}_{1}}\right)}{(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{|\mathcal{A}|R^{2}_{1}})}{(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{|\mathcal{A}|R^{2}_{1}})}{(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{|\mathcal{A}|R^{2}_{1}})}}.

Therefore, the algorithm terminates in
O(1/\epsilon^{2})
rounds. By
Theorem

A.1
, each round requires
\tilde{O}(1/\epsilon^{4})
samples to estimate
\hat{D}
and
\hat{G}
, resulting in an overall sample complexity of
\tilde{O}(1/\epsilon^{6})
.
∎

Similarly to
Lemma

6.1
, we can apply the patching in
Algorithm

2
implicitly.

Appendix A
Useful Lemmas

Lemma A.1

(Property of Traces and Frobenius norms)
.

For any matrix
A\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times n}
, the Frobenius norm is defined as

{\mathchoice{\left\|A\right\|}{\|A\|}{\|A\|}{\|A\|}}_{F}=\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{m}\sum_{j=1}^{n}a_{ij}^{2}}.

We have

1.

\Tr(AA^{T})=\Tr(A^{T}A)={\mathchoice{\left\|A\right\|}{\|A\|}{\|A\|}{\|A\|}}_{F}^{2}
.

2.

When
A,B
are square matrices,
\Tr(AB)\leq\sqrt{\Tr(AA^{T})\cdot\Tr(BB^{T})}={\mathchoice{\left\|A\right\|}{\|A\|}{\|A\|}{\|A\|}}_{F}{\mathchoice{\left\|B\right\|}{\|B\|}{\|B\|}{\|B\|}}_{F}
.

3.

Frobenius norm has submultiplicative property, that is, for any matrix
A,B
,

{\mathchoice{\left\|AB\right\|}{\|AB\|}{\|AB\|}{\|AB\|}}_{F}\leq{\mathchoice{\left\|A\right\|}{\|A\|}{\|A\|}{\|A\|}}_{F}{\mathchoice{\left\|B\right\|}{\|B\|}{\|B\|}{\|B\|}}_{F}.

Theorem A.1

(Hoeffding’s Inequality for Hilbert Spaces)
.

Let
\mathcal{H}
be a separable Hilbert space. Let
X_{1},\cdots,X_{N}
be independent random elements of
\mathcal{H}
with common mean
\mu
such that
{\mathchoice{\left\|X_{i}\right\|}{\|X_{i}\|}{\|X_{i}\|}{\|X_{i}\|}}\leq B
almost surely for any
i\in[N]
. Let
\hat{\mu}_{N}:=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}X_{i}
denote the sample mean. Then for any
\delta\in(0,1)
with probability at least
1-\delta
,

{\mathchoice{\left\|\hat{\mu}_{N}-\mu\right\|}{\|\hat{\mu}_{N}-\mu\|}{\|\hat{\mu}_{N}-\mu\|}{\|\hat{\mu}_{N}-\mu\|}}\leq 2B\sqrt{\frac{2\ln(2/\delta)}{N}}.

We will introduce some useful results for proving the uniform convergence guarantee.

Definition A.1

(Covering Numbers)
.

Let
(V,d)
be a metric space and
\Theta\subset V
. We say
\{v_{i}\}_{i=1}^{N}\subset V
is an
\epsilon
-covering of
\Theta
if
\Theta\subset\bigcup_{i=1}^{N}B(v_{i},\epsilon)
where
B(v,\epsilon):=\{u\in V:d(u,v)\leq\epsilon\}
is the closed ball of radius
\epsilon
centered at
v
. The covering number is defined as

N(\Theta,d,\epsilon):=\min\{n:\exists\epsilon\text{-covering of}~\Theta~\text{of size}~n\}

Definition A.2

(Rademacher Complexity)
.

Let
S=\{z_{1},...,z_{n}\}\subset Z
be a sample of points, and a function class
\mathcal{F}
of real-valued functions over
Z
. The Rademacher complexity of
\mathcal{F}
with respect to
S
is defined as follows:

\mathcal{R}_{S}(\mathcal{F})=\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}_{\sigma\sim\{-1,+1\}^{m}}{\mathchoice{\left[\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sigma_{i}f(z_{i})\right]}{[\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sigma_{i}f(z_{i})]}{[\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sigma_{i}f(z_{i})]}{[\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sigma_{i}f(z_{i})]}}

Theorem A.2
.

Assume that
z_{1},...,z_{m}
are i.i.d. drawn from
\mathcal{D}
, then
with probability at least
1-\delta
, we have

\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}{\mathchoice{\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f(z_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{z\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[f(z)\right]}{[f(z)]}{[f(z)]}{[f(z)]}}\right]}{[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f(z_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{z\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[f(z)\right]}{[f(z)]}{[f(z)]}{[f(z)]}}]}{[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f(z_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{z\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[f(z)\right]}{[f(z)]}{[f(z)]}{[f(z)]}}]}{[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f(z_{i})-\mathbb{E}_{z\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[f(z)\right]}{[f(z)]}{[f(z)]}{[f(z)]}}]}}\leq 2\mathbb{E}_{S\sim\mathcal{D}^{m}}{\mathchoice{\left[\mathcal{R}_{S}(\mathcal{F})\right]}{[\mathcal{R}_{S}(\mathcal{F})]}{[\mathcal{R}_{S}(\mathcal{F})]}{[\mathcal{R}_{S}(\mathcal{F})]}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(2/\delta)}{2n}}

We consider a Hilbert ball
B_{2}=\{x\in{\mathbb{R}}^{\infty}|\sum_{t}x_{i}^{2}\leq 1\}

Now we introduce the result that upper bounds the covering number of Hilbert balls under some metric induced by a probability distribution
P
. Note that under the common metric
\ell_{2}({\mathbb{R}}^{\infty})
, the covering number of the Hilbert balls is infinite. However, under the metric
d_{p}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})=\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{X\sim P}{\mathchoice{\left|\langle\theta-\theta^{\prime},X\rangle\right|}{|\langle\theta-\theta^{\prime},X\rangle|}{|\langle\theta-\theta^{\prime},X\rangle|}{|\langle\theta-\theta^{\prime},X\rangle|}}^{2}}
, the covering number is finite even in the infinite dimensional Hilbert space.

Theorem A.3

(Covering Number of Hilbert Balls
MacKay [2003]
)
.

P
is a distribution on
B_{2}
, consider the metric
d_{P}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})=\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{X\sim P}{\mathchoice{\left|\langle\theta-\theta^{\prime},X\rangle\right|}{|\langle\theta-\theta^{\prime},X\rangle|}{|\langle\theta-\theta^{\prime},X\rangle|}{|\langle\theta-\theta^{\prime},X\rangle|}}^{2}}
. There exists a universal constant
c
, such that for any
P
,
\epsilon>0
, we have

\log N(B_{2},d_{P},\epsilon)\leq\frac{c}{\epsilon^{2}}.

Let
P_{n}
be the empirical distribution, which is the uniform distribution over
z_{1},...,z_{n}
. For a function class
\mathcal{F}
, we define the metric
L_{2}^{\mathcal{F}}(P_{n})(f,f^{\prime})=\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(f(z_{i})-f^{\prime}(z_{i}))^{2}}
. Note that if you plug in
P=P_{n}
for the metric
d_{P}
in
Theorem

A.3
, then the metric
d_{P}
becomes a special case of
L_{2}^{\mathcal{F}}(P_{n})
for
f(z)=\langle\theta,z\rangle
.

Now we indroduce the Dudley’s Theorem which bounds the Rademacher complexity of a function class by its covering number.

Theorem A.4

(Localized Dudley’s Theorem)
.

Let
S=\{z_{1},...,z_{n}\}\subset Z
be a sample of points, and a function class
\mathcal{F}
of real-valued functions over
Z
. For any
\alpha\geq 0
, we have

\mathcal{R}_{S}(\mathcal{F})\leq 4\alpha+12\int_{\alpha}^{\infty}\sqrt{\frac{\log N(\mathcal{F},L_{2}^{\mathcal{F}}(P_{n}),\epsilon)}{n}}d\epsilon.

(9)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O{\mathchoice{\left(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}\log(R_{1}R_{2}n)+\log(1/\delta)}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}\log(R_{1}R_{2}n)+\log(1/\delta)}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}\log(R_{1}R_{2}n)+\log(1/\delta)}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}\log(R_{1}R_{2}n)+\log(1/\delta)}{\sqrt{n}})}}.

Note that this bound is independent of
d
, therefore holds for infinite dimension space.

To prove the theorem, recall that we define the function class
\mathcal{G}
, where each element is a function parameterized by the loss function
\ell
and
\ell^{\prime}
. The function takes a data point as input and output the loss they the agent receives when they respond based on
\ell^{\prime}
and their true loss to be
\ell
. In detail, we have

g_{l,l^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y)):=\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}{\mathchoice{\left\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}\tilde{k}_{f_{p},\ell^{\prime}}(x,a)=\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}{\mathchoice{\left\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}\frac{e^{-\beta{\mathchoice{\left\langle\ell^{\prime}_{a},p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\ell^{\prime}_{a},p(x)\rangle}{\langle\ell^{\prime}_{a},p(x)\rangle}{\langle\ell^{\prime}_{a},p(x)\rangle}}}}{\sum_{a^{\prime}=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta{\mathchoice{\left\langle\ell^{\prime}_{a^{\prime}},p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle\ell^{\prime}_{a^{\prime}},p(x)\rangle}{\langle\ell^{\prime}_{a^{\prime}},p(x)\rangle}{\langle\ell^{\prime}_{a^{\prime}},p(x)\rangle}}}}.

Now we can show that, the difference between
g_{\ell^{1},{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))
and
g_{\ell^{2},{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))
is small when
\ell^{1}\approx{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}
and
\ell^{2}\approx{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}
.

Lemma B.1
.

Consider the vector softmax function
\mathrm{softmax}(z)_{i}=\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}}}
for each coordinate
i\in[|\mathcal{A}|]
and
z\in\mathbb{R}^{|\mathcal{A}|}
, then we have

{\mathchoice{\left\|\mathrm{softmax}(z)-\mathrm{softmax}(z^{\prime})\right\|}{\|\mathrm{softmax}(z)-\mathrm{softmax}(z^{\prime})\|}{\|\mathrm{softmax}(z)-\mathrm{softmax}(z^{\prime})\|}{\|\mathrm{softmax}(z)-\mathrm{softmax}(z^{\prime})\|}}_{1}\leq\sqrt{2}\beta{\mathchoice{\left\|z-z^{\prime}\right\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}}_{2}.

Proof.

Then by mean value theorem, we know that

\mathrm{softmax}(z)-\mathrm{softmax}(z^{\prime})=\int_{t=0}^{1}\nabla\mathrm{softmax}(z^{\prime}+(z-z^{\prime})t)(z-z^{\prime})dt.

By taking the
\ell_{1}
norm, we have

{\mathchoice{\left\|\mathrm{softmax}(z)-\mathrm{softmax}(z^{\prime})\right\|}{\|\mathrm{softmax}(z)-\mathrm{softmax}(z^{\prime})\|}{\|\mathrm{softmax}(z)-\mathrm{softmax}(z^{\prime})\|}{\|\mathrm{softmax}(z)-\mathrm{softmax}(z^{\prime})\|}}_{1}\leq\int_{t=0}^{1}{\mathchoice{\left\|\nabla\mathrm{softmax}(z^{\prime}+(z-z^{\prime})t)(z-z^{\prime})\right\|}{\|\nabla\mathrm{softmax}(z^{\prime}+(z-z^{\prime})t)(z-z^{\prime})\|}{\|\nabla\mathrm{softmax}(z^{\prime}+(z-z^{\prime})t)(z-z^{\prime})\|}{\|\nabla\mathrm{softmax}(z^{\prime}+(z-z^{\prime})t)(z-z^{\prime})\|}}_{1}dt.

Let
z_{t}=z^{\prime}+(z-z^{\prime})t
and
p_{t}=\mathrm{softmax}(z_{t})
. We have
A:=\nabla\mathrm{softmax}(z_{t})=-\beta(diag(p_{t})-p_{t}p_{t}^{T})
. Then we have

\displaystyle{\mathchoice{\left\|A(z-z^{\prime})\right\|}{\|A(z-z^{\prime})\|}{\|A(z-z^{\prime})\|}{\|A(z-z^{\prime})\|}}_{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}{\mathchoice{\left|\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}a_{ij}(z_{j}-z_{j}^{\prime})\right|}{|\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}a_{ij}(z_{j}-z_{j}^{\prime})|}{|\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}a_{ij}(z_{j}-z_{j}^{\prime})|}{|\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}a_{ij}(z_{j}-z_{j}^{\prime})|}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}a_{ij}^{2}}{\mathchoice{\left\|z-z^{\prime}\right\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}}_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\beta\sqrt{(p_{i}-p_{i}^{2})^{2}+p_{i}^{2}\sum_{j\neq i}p_{j}^{2}}{\mathchoice{\left\|z-z^{\prime}\right\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\beta p_{i}\sqrt{(1-p_{i})^{2}+1}{\mathchoice{\left\|z-z^{\prime}\right\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\sqrt{2}\beta p_{i}{\mathchoice{\left\|z-z^{\prime}\right\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}}_{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{2}\beta{\mathchoice{\left\|z-z^{\prime}\right\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}}_{2}.

∎

Lemma B.2
.

Let
g(z,w):=\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}}}w_{i}

. If
{\mathchoice{\left\|w\right\|}{\|w\|}{\|w\|}{\|w\|}}_{\infty}\leq 4R_{1}R_{2}

{\mathchoice{\left|g(z,w)-g(z^{\prime},w^{\prime})\right|}{|g(z,w)-g(z^{\prime},w^{\prime})|}{|g(z,w)-g(z^{\prime},w^{\prime})|}{|g(z,w)-g(z^{\prime},w^{\prime})|}}\leq 4\sqrt{2}R_{1}R_{2}{\mathchoice{\left\|z-z^{\prime}\right\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}}_{2}+{\mathchoice{\left\|w-w^{\prime}\right\|}{\|w-w^{\prime}\|}{\|w-w^{\prime}\|}{\|w-w^{\prime}\|}}_{2}
.

\displaystyle={\mathchoice{\left|\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}}}w_{i}-\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}^{\prime}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}^{\prime}}}w_{i}^{\prime}\right|}{|\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}}}w_{i}-\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}^{\prime}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}^{\prime}}}w_{i}^{\prime}|}{|\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}}}w_{i}-\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}^{\prime}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}^{\prime}}}w_{i}^{\prime}|}{|\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}}}w_{i}-\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}^{\prime}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}^{\prime}}}w_{i}^{\prime}|}}
