z_{j}}}-\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}^{\prime}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}^{\prime}}})}}w_{i}^{\prime}}|}}

(13)

\displaystyle\leq 4R_{1}R_{2}\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}{\mathchoice{\left|\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}}}-\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}^{\prime}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}^{\prime}}}\right|}{|\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}}}-\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}^{\prime}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}^{\prime}}}|}{|\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}}}-\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}^{\prime}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}^{\prime}}}|}{|\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}}}-\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}^{\prime}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}^{\prime}}}|}}.

From
Lemma

B.1
, we have

{\mathchoice{\left|{\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}}}w_{i}^{\prime}-\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}^{\prime}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}^{\prime}}}w_{i}^{\prime}}\right|}{|{\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}}}w_{i}^{\prime}-\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}^{\prime}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}^{\prime}}}w_{i}^{\prime}}|}{|{\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}}}w_{i}^{\prime}-\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}^{\prime}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}^{\prime}}}w_{i}^{\prime}}|}{|{\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}}}w_{i}^{\prime}-\sum_{i=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\frac{e^{-\beta z_{i}^{\prime}}}{\sum_{j=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}e^{-\beta z_{j}^{\prime}}}w_{i}^{\prime}}|}}\leq 4\sqrt{2}\beta R_{1}R_{2}{\mathchoice{\left\|z-z^{\prime}\right\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}}_{2}.

(14)

Therefore, we know

{\mathchoice{\left|g(z,w)-g(z^{\prime},w^{\prime})\right|}{|g(z,w)-g(z^{\prime},w^{\prime})|}{|g(z,w)-g(z^{\prime},w^{\prime})|}{|g(z,w)-g(z^{\prime},w^{\prime})|}}\leq 4\sqrt{2}\beta R_{1}R_{2}{\mathchoice{\left\|z-z^{\prime}\right\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}{\|z-z^{\prime}\|}}_{2}+{\mathchoice{\left\|w-w^{\prime}\right\|}{\|w-w^{\prime}\|}{\|w-w^{\prime}\|}{\|w-w^{\prime}\|}}_{2}.

∎

Lemma B.3
.

There exists a constant
C
, such that for any
x,y
, we have

{\mathchoice{\left|g_{\ell^{1},{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))-g_{\ell^{2},{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))\right|}{|g_{\ell^{1},{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))-g_{\ell^{2},{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))|}{|g_{\ell^{1},{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))-g_{\ell^{2},{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))|}{|g_{\ell^{1},{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))-g_{\ell^{2},{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))|}}^{2}\leq C{\mathchoice{\left(\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle{r_{{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(a)}-r_{{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(a),p(x)\rangle^{2}+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle r_{\ell^{1}}(a)-r_{\ell^{2}}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle^{2}\right)}{(\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle{r_{{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(a)}-r_{{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(a),p(x)\rangle^{2}+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle r_{\ell^{1}}(a)-r_{\ell^{2}}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle^{2})}{(\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle{r_{{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(a)}-r_{{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(a),p(x)\rangle^{2}+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle r_{\ell^{1}}(a)-r_{\ell^{2}}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle^{2})}{(\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle{r_{{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(a)}-r_{{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(a),p(x)\rangle^{2}+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle r_{\ell^{1}}(a)-r_{\ell^{2}}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle^{2})}}.

Proof.

Because the norm of
p(x)
and
\phi(y)
is bounded by
R_{2}
, and the norm of loss vector
r_{\ell}(a)
is bounded by
R_{1}
, by
Lemma

B.2
, we know

{\mathchoice{\left|g_{\ell^{1},{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(x,y)-g_{\ell^{2},{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))\right|}{|g_{\ell^{1},{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(x,y)-g_{\ell^{2},{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))|}{|g_{\ell^{1},{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(x,y)-g_{\ell^{2},{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))|}{|g_{\ell^{1},{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(x,y)-g_{\ell^{2},{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))|}}\leq 4\sqrt{2}\beta R_{1}R_{2}\sqrt{\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle r_{{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(a)-r_{{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(a),p(x)\rangle^{2}}+\sqrt{\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle r_{\ell^{1}}(a)-r_{\ell^{2}}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle^{2}}.

(15)

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\mathchoice{\left|g_{\ell^{1},{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))-g_{\ell^{2},{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))\right|}{|g_{\ell^{1},{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))-g_{\ell^{2},{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))|}{|g_{\ell^{1},{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))-g_{\ell^{2},{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))|}{|g_{\ell^{1},{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))-g_{\ell^{2},{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))|}}^{2}

(16)

\displaystyle\leq{\mathchoice{\left(4\sqrt{2}\beta R_{1}R_{2}\sqrt{\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle r_{{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(a)-r_{{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(a),p(x)\rangle^{2}}+\sqrt{\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle r_{\ell^{1}}(a)-r_{\ell^{2}}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle^{2}}\right)}{(4\sqrt{2}\beta R_{1}R_{2}\sqrt{\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle r_{{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(a)-r_{{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(a),p(x)\rangle^{2}}+\sqrt{\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle r_{\ell^{1}}(a)-r_{\ell^{2}}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle^{2}})}{(4\sqrt{2}\beta R_{1}R_{2}\sqrt{\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle r_{{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(a)-r_{{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(a),p(x)\rangle^{2}}+\sqrt{\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle r_{\ell^{1}}(a)-r_{\ell^{2}}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle^{2}})}{(4\sqrt{2}\beta R_{1}R_{2}\sqrt{\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle r_{{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(a)-r_{{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(a),p(x)\rangle^{2}}+\sqrt{\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle r_{\ell^{1}}(a)-r_{\ell^{2}}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle^{2}})}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 64\beta^{2}R_{1}^{2}R_{2}^{2}\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle r_{{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(a)-r_{{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(a),p(x)\rangle^{2}+2\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle r_{\ell^{1}}(a)-r_{\ell^{2}}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle^{2}.

We can set
C=\max\{64\beta^{2}R_{1}^{2}R_{2}^{2},2\}
and thus the lemma is proved.
∎

Lemma B.4
.

Consider
\mathcal{G}=\{g_{\ell,\ell^{\prime}}|\forall a\in\mathcal{A},\ell(a,\cdot)\in\mathcal{H},{\mathchoice{\left\|\ell(a,\cdot)\right\|}{\|\ell(a,\cdot)\|}{\|\ell(a,\cdot)\|}{\|\ell(a,\cdot)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}\leq R_{1}\}
, then we have

\log N(\mathcal{G},L_{2}^{\mathcal{G}}(P_{n}),\epsilon)\leq O{\mathchoice{\left(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{3}\beta^{4}R_{1}^{6}R_{2}^{6}}{\epsilon^{2}}\right)}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{3}\beta^{4}R_{1}^{6}R_{2}^{6}}{\epsilon^{2}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{3}\beta^{4}R_{1}^{6}R_{2}^{6}}{\epsilon^{2}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{3}\beta^{4}R_{1}^{6}R_{2}^{6}}{\epsilon^{2}})}}.

Proof.

By
Lemma

B.3
, we know

\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\mathchoice{\left|g_{\ell^{1},{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))-g_{\ell^{2},{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))\right|}{|g_{\ell^{1},{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))-g_{\ell^{2},{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))|}{|g_{\ell^{1},{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))-g_{\ell^{2},{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))|}{|g_{\ell^{1},{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))-g_{\ell^{2},{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))|}}^{2}\leq C\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\mathchoice{\left(\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle r_{{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(a)-r_{{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(a),p(x_{i})\rangle^{2}+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle r_{\ell^{1}}(a)-r_{\ell^{2}}(a),\phi(y_{i})-p(x_{i})\rangle^{2}\right)}{(\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle r_{{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(a)-r_{{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(a),p(x_{i})\rangle^{2}+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle r_{\ell^{1}}(a)-r_{\ell^{2}}(a),\phi(y_{i})-p(x_{i})\rangle^{2})}{(\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle r_{{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(a)-r_{{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(a),p(x_{i})\rangle^{2}+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle r_{\ell^{1}}(a)-r_{\ell^{2}}(a),\phi(y_{i})-p(x_{i})\rangle^{2})}{(\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle r_{{\ell^{1}}^{\prime}}(a)-r_{{\ell^{2}}^{\prime}}(a),p(x_{i})\rangle^{2}+\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\langle r_{\ell^{1}}(a)-r_{\ell^{2}}(a),\phi(y_{i})-p(x_{i})\rangle^{2})}}.

The high level idea is to construct covers for
2|\mathcal{A}|
Hilbert balls, then we can bound the right-hand side. Then, the Cartisan product of these covers would be a
\epsilon
cover for the function class
\mathcal{G}
.

By
Theorem

A.3
, Let
L_{a}
be the smallest
\frac{\epsilon}{2|\mathcal{A}|C}
-cover of
\Theta_{r_{\ell}(a)}:=\{r_{\ell}(a)|r_{\ell}(a)\in\mathcal{H},{\mathchoice{\left\|r_{\ell}(a)\right\|}{\|r_{\ell}(a)\|}{\|r_{\ell}(a)\|}{\|r_{\ell}(a)\|}}_{\mathcal{H}}\leq R_{1}\}
, we have
\log|L_{a}|\leq O{\mathchoice{\left(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{2}C^{2}R_{1}^{2}R_{2}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}\right)}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{2}C^{2}R_{1}^{2}R_{2}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{2}C^{2}R_{1}^{2}R_{2}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{2}C^{2}R_{1}^{2}R_{2}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}})}}
.
Therefore,
L:=\prod_{a\in\mathcal{A}}L_{a}\times\prod_{a\in\mathcal{A}}L_{a}
would become a
\epsilon
-cover of
\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{H}}\times\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{H}}
under the metric
L_{2}^{\mathcal{G}}(P_{n})
.
We have

\log|L|=2\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\log|L_{a}|=O{\mathchoice{\left(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{3}C^{2}R_{1}^{2}R_{2}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}\right)}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{3}C^{2}R_{1}^{2}R_{2}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{3}C^{2}R_{1}^{2}R_{2}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{3}C^{2}R_{1}^{2}R_{2}^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}})}}.

As we know
C=\max\{64\beta^{2}R_{1}^{2}R_{2}^{2},2\}
, we know

\log|L|=O{\mathchoice{\left(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{3}\beta^{4}R_{1}^{6}R_{2}^{6}}{\epsilon^{2}}\right)}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{3}\beta^{4}R_{1}^{6}R_{2}^{6}}{\epsilon^{2}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{3}\beta^{4}R_{1}^{6}R_{2}^{6}}{\epsilon^{2}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{3}\beta^{4}R_{1}^{6}R_{2}^{6}}{\epsilon^{2}})}}.

∎

Now we prove
Theorem

B.1
.

Proof.

Recalling
g_{l,l^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))=\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}{\mathchoice{\left\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}\tilde{k}_{f_{p},\ell^{\prime}}(x,a)
, we know

\displaystyle{\mathchoice{\left|g_{l,l^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))\right|}{|g_{l,l^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))|}{|g_{l,l^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))|}{|g_{l,l^{\prime}}(p(x),\phi(y))|}}

\displaystyle={\mathchoice{\left|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}{\mathchoice{\left\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}\tilde{k}_{f_{p},\ell^{\prime}}(x,a)\right|}{|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}{\mathchoice{\left\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}\tilde{k}_{f_{p},\ell^{\prime}}(x,a)|}{|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}{\mathchoice{\left\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}\tilde{k}_{f_{p},\ell^{\prime}}(x,a)|}{|\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}{\mathchoice{\left\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}\tilde{k}_{f_{p},\ell^{\prime}}(x,a)|}}

(17)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\tilde{k}_{f_{p},\ell^{\prime}}(x,a){\mathchoice{\left|{\mathchoice{\left\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}\right|}{|{\mathchoice{\left\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}|}{|{\mathchoice{\left\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}|}{|{\mathchoice{\left\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\right\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}{\langle r_{\ell}(a),\phi(y)-p(x)\rangle}}|}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{a=1}^{|\mathcal{A}|}\tilde{k}_{f_{p},\ell^{\prime}}(x,a)2R_{1}R_{2}

\displaystyle=2R_{1}R_{2}.

Therefore, when
\epsilon\geq 2R_{1}R_{2}
, we have
\log N(\mathcal{G},L_{2}^{\mathcal{G}}(P_{n}),\epsilon)=\log(1)=0

From
Theorem

A.4
, we know that

\mathcal{R}_{S}(\mathcal{G})\leq 4\alpha+12\int_{\alpha}^{\infty}\sqrt{\frac{\log N(\mathcal{G},L_{2}^{\mathcal{G}}(P_{n}),\epsilon)}{n}}d\epsilon=4\alpha+12\int_{\alpha}^{2R_{1}R_{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\log N(\mathcal{G},L_{2}^{\mathcal{G}}(P_{n}),\epsilon)}{n}}d\epsilon.

Plugging in
\log N(\mathcal{G},L_{2}^{\mathcal{G}}(P_{n}),\epsilon)=O{\mathchoice{\left(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{3}\beta^{4}R_{1}^{6}R_{2}^{6}}{\epsilon^{2}}\right)}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{3}\beta^{4}R_{1}^{6}R_{2}^{6}}{\epsilon^{2}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{3}\beta^{4}R_{1}^{6}R_{2}^{6}}{\epsilon^{2}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{3}\beta^{4}R_{1}^{6}R_{2}^{6}}{\epsilon^{2}})}}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{S}(\mathcal{G})

\displaystyle\leq 4\alpha+O{\mathchoice{\left(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}}\int_{\alpha}^{2R_{1}R_{2}}\frac{1}{\epsilon}d\epsilon

(18)

\displaystyle=4\alpha+O{\mathchoice{\left(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}]\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}]\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}]\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}]\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}}(\log{2R_{1}R_{2}}-\log\alpha).

Without loss of generality we set
\alpha=\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}}
. If
\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}}>2R_{1}R_{2}
, we have
\mathcal{R}_{S}(\mathcal{G})\leq 4\alpha\leq O{\mathchoice{\left(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}}
. If
\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}}\leq 2R_{1}R_{2}
,

\mathcal{R}_{S}(\mathcal{G})\leq O{\mathchoice{\left(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}}+O{\mathchoice{\left(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}}(\log{2R_{1}R_{2}}-\log{\mathchoice{\left(O{\mathchoice{\left(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}}\right)}{(O{\mathchoice{\left(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}})}{(O{\mathchoice{\left(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}})}{(O{\mathchoice{\left(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}}{\sqrt{n}})}})}}=O{\mathchoice{\left(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}\log(R_{1}R_{2}n)}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}\log(R_{1}R_{2}n)}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}\log(R_{1}R_{2}n)}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}\log(R_{1}R_{2}n)}{\sqrt{n}})}}.

Then by
Theorem

A.2
, we know with at least probability
1-\delta/2

\displaystyle\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}}{\mathchoice{\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))-\mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[g(p(x),\phi(y))\right]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}}\right]}{[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))-\mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[g(p(x),\phi(y))\right]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}}]}{[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))-\mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[g(p(x),\phi(y))\right]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}}]}{[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))-\mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[g(p(x),\phi(y))\right]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}}]}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}_{S\sim\mathcal{D}^{n}}{\mathchoice{\left[\mathcal{R}_{S}(\mathcal{G})\right]}{[\mathcal{R}_{S}(\mathcal{G})]}{[\mathcal{R}_{S}(\mathcal{G})]}{[\mathcal{R}_{S}(\mathcal{G})]}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(2/\delta)}{2n}}

\displaystyle\leq O{\mathchoice{\left(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}\log(R_{1}R_{2}n)+\log(1/\delta)}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}\log(R_{1}R_{2}n)+\log(1/\delta)}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}\log(R_{1}R_{2}n)+\log(1/\delta)}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}\log(R_{1}R_{2}n)+\log(1/\delta)}{\sqrt{n}})}}.

Similarly we can bound the Rademacher Complexity of function class
-\mathcal{G}:=\{-g_{\ell,\ell^{\prime}}\mid\ell,\ell^{\prime}\in\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{H}}\}
, and have with probability
1-\delta/2

\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}}{\mathchoice{\left[\mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[g(p(x),\phi(y))\right]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))\right]}{[\mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[g(p(x),\phi(y))\right]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))]}{[\mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[g(p(x),\phi(y))\right]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))]}{[\mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[g(p(x),\phi(y))\right]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}}-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))]}}\leq O{\mathchoice{\left(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}\log(R_{1}R_{2}n)+\log(1/\delta)}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}\log(R_{1}R_{2}n)+\log(1/\delta)}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}\log(R_{1}R_{2}n)+\log(1/\delta)}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}\log(R_{1}R_{2}n)+\log(1/\delta)}{\sqrt{n}})}}.

Putting them together, we have

{\mathchoice{\left|\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}}{\mathchoice{\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))-\mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[g(p(x),\phi(y))\right]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}}\right]}{[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))-\mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[g(p(x),\phi(y))\right]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}}]}{[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))-\mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[g(p(x),\phi(y))\right]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}}]}{[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))-\mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[g(p(x),\phi(y))\right]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}}]}}\right|}{|\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}}{\mathchoice{\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))-\mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[g(p(x),\phi(y))\right]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}}\right]}{[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))-\mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[g(p(x),\phi(y))\right]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}}]}{[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))-\mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[g(p(x),\phi(y))\right]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}}]}{[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))-\mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[g(p(x),\phi(y))\right]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}}]}}|}{|\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}}{\mathchoice{\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))-\mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[g(p(x),\phi(y))\right]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}}\right]}{[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))-\mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[g(p(x),\phi(y))\right]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}}]}{[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))-\mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[g(p(x),\phi(y))\right]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}}]}{[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))-\mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[g(p(x),\phi(y))\right]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}}]}}|}{|\sup_{g\in\mathcal{G}}{\mathchoice{\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))-\mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[g(p(x),\phi(y))\right]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}}\right]}{[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))-\mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[g(p(x),\phi(y))\right]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}}]}{[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))-\mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[g(p(x),\phi(y))\right]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}}]}{[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}g(p(x_{i}),\phi(y_{i}))-\mathbb{E}_{(x,y)\sim\mathcal{D}}{\mathchoice{\left[g(p(x),\phi(y))\right]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}{[g(p(x),\phi(y))]}}]}}|}}\leq O{\mathchoice{\left(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}\log(R_{1}R_{2}n)+\log(1/\delta)}{\sqrt{n}}\right)}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}\log(R_{1}R_{2}n)+\log(1/\delta)}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}\log(R_{1}R_{2}n)+\log(1/\delta)}{\sqrt{n}})}{(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|^{\frac{3}{2}}\beta^{2}R_{1}^{3}R_{2}^{3}\log(R_{1}R_{2}n)+\log(1/\delta)}{\sqrt{n}})}}

with probability
1-\delta
.
∎
